                float f1 = 1.0f / event.red;
                if (f1 > 1.0f / event.green) {
                    f1 = 1.0f / event.green;
                }
                if (f1 > 1.0f / event.blue) {
                    f1 = 1.0f / event.blue;
                }
                event.red = event.red * (1.0f - f2) + event.red * f1 * f2;
                event.green = event.green * (1.0f - f2) + event.green * f1 * f2;
                event.blue = event.blue * (1.0f - f2) + event.blue * f1 * f2;

    @SubscribeEvent
    public void onLivingFall(LivingFallEvent event) {

import java.util.function.BiFunction;
import java.util.function.BiPredicate;
import java.util.function.Predicate;
import java.util.function.Supplier;
import org.apache.logging.log4j.LogManager;
import org.apache.logging.log4j.Logger;
import org.objectweb.asm.ClassReader;
import org.objectweb.asm.ClassVisitor;
import org.objectweb.asm.ClassWriter;
import org.objectweb.asm.tree.ClassNode;
import org.objectweb.asm.tree.FieldNode;
import org.objectweb.asm.tree.InsnList;
import org.objectweb.asm.tree.MethodNode;

public class SHClassVisitor {
    public static final Logger LOGGER = LogManager.getLogger((String)"Fisk's Superheroes/ASM");
    public final String classPath;
    public final String unobfClass;
    private BiFunction<MethodNode, Integer, Integer> methodOperator = (m, i) -> i;
    private BiFunction<FieldNode, Integer, Integer> fieldOperator = (f, i) -> i;
    private int methodPatchers;
    private int fieldPatchers;
    private List<MethodNode> methodList = new ArrayList<MethodNode>();
    private List<FieldNode> fieldList = new ArrayList<FieldNode>();
    private List<Supplier<String>> interfaceList = new ArrayList<Supplier<String>>();
    private Side sideOnly;
    private boolean writeClassFile = true;

    public SHClassVisitor(String classPath) {
        this.classPath = classPath;
        this.unobfClass = classPath.substring(classPath.lastIndexOf(46) + 1);
    }

    public byte[] transform(String name, String transformedName, byte[] bytes) throws Exception {
        if (transformedName.equals(this.classPath)) {
            LOGGER.debug(String.format("Patching Class %s (%s)", this.unobfClass, name));
            ClassReader reader = new ClassReader(bytes);
            ClassNode node = new ClassNode();
            reader.accept((ClassVisitor)node, 0);
            boolean success = this.processMethods(node.methods) && this.processFields(node.fields);
            this.interfaceList.forEach(t -> node.interfaces.add(t.get()));
            ClassWriter cw = new ClassWriter(1);
            node.accept((ClassVisitor)cw);
            if (success) {
                LOGGER.debug(String.format("Patching Class %s done", this.unobfClass));
            } else {
                LOGGER.error(String.format("Patching Class %s FAILED!", this.unobfClass));
            }
            if (this.writeClassFile) {
                SHClassVisitor.writeClassFile(cw, String.format("%s (%s)", this.unobfClass, name));
            }
            return cw.toByteArray();
        }
        return bytes;
    }

    public boolean processMethods(List<MethodNode> methods) {
        int i = 0;
        for (MethodNode method : methods) {
            i = this.methodOperator.apply(method, i);
        }
        methods.addAll(this.methodList);
        return i == this.methodPatchers;
    }

    public boolean processFields(List<FieldNode> fields) {
        int i = 0;
        for (FieldNode field : fields) {
            i = this.fieldOperator.apply(field, i);
        }
        fields.addAll(this.fieldList);
        return i == this.fieldPatchers;
    }

    public void sideOnly(Side side) {
        this.sideOnly = side;
    }

    public void writeClassFile(boolean result) {
        this.writeClassFile = result;
    }

    private boolean checkSide() {
        return this.sideOnly != null && this.sideOnly != FMLLaunchHandler.side();
    }

    public void patchMethod(String name, String devName, String desc, String devDesc, BiPredicate<MethodNode, InsnList> func) {
        if (this.checkSide()) {
            return;
        }
        BiFunction<MethodNode, Integer, Integer> op = this.methodOperator;
        String obfName = SHClassTransformer.map(name, devName);
        String obfDesc = SHClassTransformer.map(desc, devDesc);
        ++this.methodPatchers;
        this.methodOperator = (m, i) -> {
            if (m.name.equals(obfName) && m.desc.equals(obfDesc)) {
                InsnList out = new InsnList();
                LOGGER.debug("\tPatching method " + devName + " in " + this.unobfClass);
                if (func.test((MethodNode)m, out)) {
                    m.instructions.clear();
                    m.instructions.add(out);
                    i = i + 1;
                } else {
                    LOGGER.error(String.format("Patching method %s in %s FAILED!", devName, this.unobfClass));
                }
            }
            return (Integer)op.apply((MethodNode)m, (Integer)i);
        };
    }

    public void addMethod(MethodNode method) {
        if (this.checkSide()) {
            return;
        }
        this.methodList.add(method);
    }

    public void patchField(String name, String devName, String desc, String devDesc, Predicate<FieldNode> func) {
        if (this.checkSide()) {
            return;
        }
        BiFunction<FieldNode, Integer, Integer> op = this.fieldOperator;
        String obfName = SHClassTransformer.map(name, devName);
        String obfDesc = SHClassTransformer.map(desc, devDesc);
        ++this.fieldPatchers;
        this.fieldOperator = (f, i) -> {
            if (f.name.equals(obfName) && f.desc.equals(obfDesc)) {
                LOGGER.debug("\tPatching field " + devName + " in " + this.unobfClass);
                if (func.test((FieldNode)f)) {
                    i = i + 1;
                } else {
                    LOGGER.error(String.format("Patching field %s in %s FAILED!", devName, this.unobfClass));
                }
            }
            return (Integer)op.apply((FieldNode)f, (Integer)i);
        };
    }

    public void addField(FieldNode field) {
        if (this.checkSide()) {
            return;
        }
        this.fieldList.add(field);
    }

    public void addInterface(Supplier<String> classPath) {
        if (this.checkSide()) {
            return;
        }
        this.interfaceList.add(classPath);
    }

    public static void writeClassFile(ClassWriter cw, String name) {
        try {
            File outDir = new File("debug/");
            outDir.mkdirs();
            DataOutputStream dout = new DataOutputStream(new FileOutputStream(new File(outDir, name + ".class")));
            dout.write(cw.toByteArray());
            dout.flush();
            dout.close();
        }
        catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
